  2056 |   function isChallengerChatActive(fixture = {}) {
  2057 |     const status = String(fixture?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  2058 | 
  2059 |     if (
  2060 |       status.includes("cancel") ||
  2061 |       status.includes("rejected") ||
  2062 |       status.includes("declined")
  2063 |     ) {
  2064 |       return false;
  2065 |     }
  2066 | 
  2067 |     const dateText = String(fixture?.proposedDate || "").trim();
  2068 |     if (!dateText) return true;
  2069 | 
  2070 |     const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
  2071 |     const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
  2072 |     const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
  2073 | 
  2074 |     if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
  2075 | 
  2076 |     return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();
  2077 |   }
  2078 | 
  2079 |   useEffect(() => {
  2080 |     if (!activeClubId) {
  2081 |       setChallengerChatFixture(null);
  2082 |       return;
  2083 |     }
  2084 | 
  2085 |     const q = query(
  2086 |       collection(db, "clubs", activeClubId, "fixtures"),
  2087 |       orderBy("createdAtMs", "desc"),
  2088 |       limit(8)
  2089 |     );
  2090 | 
  2091 |     const unsub = onSnapshot(q, (snap) => {
  2092 |       const fixtures = snap.docs
  2093 |         .map((d) => ({
  2094 |           id: d.id,
  2095 |           ...(d.data() || {}),
  2096 |         }))
  2097 |         .filter((fixture) => fixture?.source === "club_challenge")
  2098 |         .filter((fixture) => isChallengerChatActive(fixture))
  2099 |         .filter((fixture) =>
  2100 |           [fixture.homeClubId, fixture.awayClubId]
  2101 |             .map((value) => String(value || ""))
  2102 |             .includes(String(activeClubId || ""))
  2103 |         )
  2104 |         .sort((a, b) => {
  2105 |           const aTime = new Date(`${a.proposedDate || "2999-12-31"}T${a.proposedKickoff || "23:59"}:00`).getTime();
  2106 |           const bTime = new Date(`${b.proposedDate || "2999-12-31"}T${b.proposedKickoff || "23:59"}:00`).getTime();
  2107 |           return aTime - bTime;
  2108 |         });
  2109 | 
  2110 |       setChallengerChatFixture(fixtures[0] || null);
  2111 |     });
  2112 | 
  2113 |     return () => unsub();
  2114 |   }, [activeClubId]);
  2115 |

   194 | function toTitleCase(name) {
   195 |   return String(name || "")
   196 |     .trim()
   197 |     .toLowerCase()
   198 |     .split(/\s+/)
   199 |     .filter(Boolean)
   200 |     .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1))
   201 |     .join(" ");
   202 | }
   203 | 
   204 | function slugFromName(name) {
   205 |   return String(name || "")
   206 |     .toLowerCase()
   207 |     .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
   208 |     .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, "");
   209 | }

   211 | 
   212 | function normalizeWhatsAppNumber(value) {
   213 |   const raw = String(value || "").trim();
   214 |   if (!raw) return "";
   215 |   if (raw.startsWith("+")) {
   216 |     return "+" + raw.slice(1).replace(/\D/g, "");
   217 |   }
   218 |   return raw.replace(/\D/g, "");
   219 | }
   220 | 
   221 | function looksLikeWhatsAppNumber(value) {
   222 |   const normalized = normalizeWhatsAppNumber(value);
   223 |   const digits = normalized.replace(/\D/g, "");
   224 |   return digits.length >= 9 && digits.length <= 15;
   225 | }

   228 | function fileToDataUrl(file) {
   229 |   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   230 |     const reader = new FileReader();
   231 |     reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
   232 |     reader.onerror = reject;
   233 |     reader.readAsDataURL(file);
   234 |   });
   235 | }

   237 | async function makeLandscapePhotoDataUrl(file, width = 900, height = 506) {
   238 |   const raw = await fileToDataUrl(file);
   239 | 
   240 |   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   241 |     const img = new Image();
   242 |     img.onload = () => {
   243 |       try {
   244 |         const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
   245 |         canvas.width = width;
   246 |         canvas.height = height;
   247 |         const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
   248 | 
   249 |         if (!ctx) {
   250 |           resolve(raw);
   251 |           return;
   252 |         }
   253 | 
   254 |         ctx.fillStyle = "#020617";
   255 |         ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
   256 | 
   257 |         const scale = Math.max(width / img.width, height / img.height);
   258 |         const drawW = img.width * scale;
   259 |         const drawH = img.height * scale;
   260 |         const dx = (width - drawW) / 2;
   261 |         const dy = (height - drawH) / 2;
   262 | 
   263 |         ctx.drawImage(img, dx, dy, drawW, drawH);
   264 |         resolve(canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.72));
   265 |       } catch (err) {
   266 |         reject(err);
   267 |       }
   268 |     };
   269 |     img.onerror = reject;
   270 |     img.src = raw;
   271 |   });
   272 | }

  2056 |   function isChallengerChatActive(fixture = {}) {
  2057 |     const status = String(fixture?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  2058 | 
  2059 |     if (
  2060 |       status.includes("cancel") ||
  2061 |       status.includes("rejected") ||
  2062 |       status.includes("declined")
  2063 |     ) {
  2064 |       return false;
  2065 |     }

  2067 |     const dateText = String(fixture?.proposedDate || "").trim();
  2068 |     if (!dateText) return true;
  2069 | 
  2070 |     const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
  2071 |     const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
  2072 |     const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
  2073 | 
  2074 |     if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
  2075 | 
  2076 |     return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();

  2671 |             {
  2672 |               status: "acknowledged",
  2673 |               acknowledgedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2674 |               acknowledgedAtMs: Date.now(),
  2675 |             }
  2676 |           );
  2677 |         } catch (err) {
  2678 |           console.error("[EntryPage] Failed acknowledging challenge notice:", err);
  2679 |           window.alert("Could not close this challenge notice just now.");
  2680 |           return;
  2681 |         }
  2682 |       }
  2683 | 
  2684 |       setChallengeNoticeAlerts((prev) =>
  2685 |         prev.filter((item) => item.noticeDocId !== notice.payload?.noticeDocId)
  2686 |       );
  2687 |       return;
  2688 |     }
  2689 | 
  2690 |     if (notice.type === "new_player") {
  2691 |       return;
  2692 |     }
  2693 |   };
  2694 | 
  2695 |   /* FANM PLATFORM PAIRING ACCEPTANCE */
  2696 | 
  2697 |   const acceptPlatformPairingChallenge = async (challenge) => {
  2698 |     const pairingId = String(
  2699 |       challenge?.platformPairingId ||
  2700 |       challenge?.challengeId ||
  2701 |       ""
  2702 |     ).trim();
  2703 | 
  2704 |     if (!pairingId || !activeClubId) return;
  2705 | 
  2706 |     const centralRef = doc(db, "clubChallenges", pairingId);
  2707 | 
  2708 |     try {
  2709 |       await runTransaction(db, async (transaction) => {
  2710 |         const centralSnap = await transaction.get(centralRef);
  2711 | 
  2712 |         const pairing = {
  2713 |           ...(challenge || {}),
  2714 |           ...(centralSnap.exists()
  2715 |             ? centralSnap.data()
  2716 |             : {}),
  2717 |         };
  2718 | 
  2719 |         const homeClubId = String(
  2720 |           pairing?.homeClubId ||
  2721 |           pairing?.challengerClubId ||
  2722 |           ""
  2723 |         ).trim();
  2724 | 
  2725 |         const awayClubId = String(
  2726 |           pairing?.awayClubId ||
  2727 |           pairing?.targetClubId ||
  2728 |           ""
  2729 |         ).trim();
  2730 | 
  2731 |         if (
  2732 |           !homeClubId ||
  2733 |           !awayClubId ||
  2734 |           ![homeClubId, awayClubId].includes(activeClubId)
  2735 |         ) {
  2736 |           throw new Error(
  2737 |             "Current club is not part of this platform pairing."
  2738 |           );
  2739 |         }
  2740 | 
  2741 |         const homeClubName =
  2742 |           pairing?.homeClubName ||
  2743 |           pairing?.challengerClubName ||
  2744 |           "Home Club";
  2745 | 
  2746 |         const awayClubName =
  2747 |           pairing?.awayClubName ||
  2748 |           pairing?.targetClubName ||
  2749 |           "Away Club";
  2750 | 
  2751 |         const previousResponses = {
  2752 |           ...(pairing?.responses || {}),
  2753 |         };
  2754 | 
  2755 |         const nextResponses = {
  2756 |           ...previousResponses,
  2757 |           [activeClubId]: "accepted",
  2758 |         };
  2759 | 
  2760 |         const homeAccepted =
  2761 |           nextResponses[homeClubId] === "accepted";
  2762 | 
  2763 |         const awayAccepted =
  2764 |           nextResponses[awayClubId] === "accepted";
  2765 | 
  2766 |         const acceptedClubIds = [
  2767 |           homeAccepted ? homeClubId : "",
  2768 |           awayAccepted ? awayClubId : "",
  2769 |         ].filter(Boolean);
  2770 | 
  2771 |         const bothAccepted =
  2772 |           homeAccepted && awayAccepted;
  2773 | 
  2774 |         const fixtureId = `challenge_${pairingId}`;
  2775 |         const nowMs = Date.now();
  2776 | 
  2777 |         const responsePatch = {
  2778 |           responses: nextResponses,
  2779 |           acceptedClubIds,
  2780 |           homeAccepted,
  2781 |           awayAccepted,
  2782 | 
  2783 |           status: bothAccepted
  2784 |             ? "accepted"
  2785 |             : "awaiting_other_club",
  2786 | 
  2787 |           fixtureStatus: bothAccepted
  2788 |             ? "fixture_created_automatically"
  2789 |             : "awaiting_both_clubs",
  2790 | 
  2791 |           updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2792 |           updatedAtMs: nowMs,
  2793 | 
  2794 |           ...(bothAccepted ? { fixtureId } : {}),
  2795 |         };
  2796 | 
  2797 |         transaction.set(
  2798 |           centralRef,
  2799 |           responsePatch,
  2800 |           { merge: true }
  2801 |         );
  2802 | 
  2803 |         transaction.set(
  2804 |           doc(
  2805 |             db,
  2806 |             "clubs",
  2807 |             activeClubId,
  2808 |             "incomingChallenges",
  2809 |             pairingId
  2810 |           ),
  2811 |           {
  2812 |             ...responsePatch,
  2813 |             respondedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2814 |             respondedAtMs: nowMs,
  2815 |           },
  2816 |           { merge: true }
  2817 |         );
  2818 | 
  2819 |         if (!bothAccepted) {
  2820 |           return;
  2821 |         }
  2822 | 
  2823 |         const participatingClubIds = [
  2824 |           homeClubId,
  2825 |           awayClubId,
  2826 |         ];
  2827 | 
  2828 |         const fixturePayload = {
  2829 |           fixtureId,
  2830 | 
  2831 |           source: "platform_pairing",
  2832 |           challengeId: pairingId,
  2833 |           platformPairingId: pairingId,
  2834 |           platformInstigated: true,
  2835 | 
  2836 |           status: "confirmed",
  2837 |           signupStatus: "open",
  2838 | 
  2839 |           homeClubId,
  2840 |           homeClubName,
  2841 |           homeClubLogo:
  2842 |             pairing?.homeClubLogo ||
  2843 |             pairing?.challengerClubLogo ||
  2844 |             "",
  2845 | 
  2846 |           awayClubId,
  2847 |           awayClubName,
  2848 |           awayClubLogo:
  2849 |             pairing?.awayClubLogo ||
  2850 |             pairing?.targetClubLogo ||
  2851 |             "",
  2852 | 
  2853 |           participatingClubIds,
  2854 | 
  2855 |           format: pairing?.format || "5v5",
  2856 | 
  2857 |           proposedDate:
  2858 |             pairing?.proposedDate || "",
  2859 | 
  2860 |           proposedKickoff:
  2861 |             pairing?.proposedKickoff || "18:30",
  2862 |

  2864 |             pairing?.venue ||
  2865 |             pairing?.proposedVenue ||
  2866 |             "Venue to be confirmed",
  2867 |

  2869 | 
  2870 |           homeAccepted: true,
  2871 |           awayAccepted: true,
  2872 |           acceptedClubIds: participatingClubIds,
  2873 | 
  2874 |           responses: {
  2875 |             [homeClubId]: "accepted",
  2876 |             [awayClubId]: "accepted",
  2877 |           },
  2878 | 
  2879 |           createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2880 |           createdAtMs:
  2881 |             Number(pairing?.createdAtMs) || nowMs,
  2882 | 
  2883 |           acceptedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2884 |           acceptedAtMs: nowMs,
  2885 | 
  2886 |           updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2887 |           updatedAtMs: nowMs,
  2888 |         };
  2889 | 
  2890 |         const acceptedPayload = {
  2891 |           ...pairing,
  2892 |           ...fixturePayload,
  2893 | 
  2894 |           status: "accepted",
  2895 |           fixtureStatus:
  2896 |             "fixture_created_automatically",
  2897 |

  2899 |           acceptedAtMs: nowMs,
  2900 |         };
  2901 | 
  2902 |         transaction.set(
  2903 |           doc(
  2904 |             db,
  2905 |             "acceptedClubChallenges",
  2906 |             pairingId
  2907 |           ),
  2908 |           acceptedPayload,
  2909 |           { merge: true }
  2910 |         );
  2911 | 
  2912 |         participatingClubIds.forEach((clubId) => {
  2913 |           const acceptedDocId =
  2914 |             `${pairingId}_${clubId}`;
  2915 | 
  2916 |           transaction.set(

  2927 |                 acceptedDocId,
  2928 |             },
  2929 |             { merge: true }
  2930 |           );
  2931 | 
  2932 |           transaction.set(
  2933 |             doc(
  2934 |               db,
  2935 |               "clubs",
  2936 |               clubId,
  2937 |               "fixtures",
  2938 |               fixtureId
  2939 |             ),
  2940 |             fixturePayload,
  2941 |             { merge: true }
  2942 |           );
  2943 | 
  2944 |           transaction.set(
  2945 |             doc(
  2946 |               db,
  2947 |               "clubs",
  2948 |               clubId,
  2949 |               "incomingChallenges",
  2950 |               pairingId

  2952 |             {
  2953 |               status: "accepted",

  2955 |               fixtureStatus:
  2956 |                 "fixture_created_automatically",
  2957 | 
  2958 |               responses: {

  2960 |                 [awayClubId]: "accepted",

  2986 |     } catch (err) {

  2989 |         err
  2990 |       );

  2994 |       );
  2995 |     }

  2998 |   const rejectPlatformPairingChallenge = async (challenge) => {
  2999 |     const pairingId = String(
  3000 |       challenge?.platformPairingId ||
  3001 |       challenge?.challengeId ||
  3002 |       ""
  3003 |     ).trim();
  3004 | 
  3005 |     if (!pairingId || !activeClubId) return;
  3006 | 
  3007 |     const homeClubId = String(
  3008 |       challenge?.homeClubId ||
  3009 |       challenge?.challengerClubId ||
  3010 |       ""
  3011 |     ).trim();
  3012 | 
  3013 |     const awayClubId = String(
  3014 |       challenge?.awayClubId ||
  3015 |       challenge?.targetClubId ||
  3016 |       ""
  3017 |     ).trim();
  3018 | 
  3019 |     const participatingClubIds = [
  3020 |       homeClubId,
  3021 |       awayClubId,
  3022 |     ].filter(Boolean);
  3023 | 
  3024 |     try {
  3025 |       const batch = writeBatch(db);
  3026 |       const nowMs = Date.now();
  3027 | 
  3028 |       batch.set(
  3029 |         doc(db, "clubChallenges", pairingId),
  3030 |         {
  3031 |           status: "rejected",
  3032 |           rejectedByClubId: activeClubId,
  3033 |           rejectedByClubName: activeClubName,
  3034 | 
  3035 |           responses: {
  3036 |             ...(challenge?.responses || {}),
  3037 |             [activeClubId]: "rejected",
  3038 |           },
  3039 | 
  3040 |           rejectedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  3041 |           rejectedAtMs: nowMs,
  3042 | 
  3043 |           updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  3044 |           updatedAtMs: nowMs,
  3045 |         },
  3046 |         { merge: true }
  3047 |       );
  3048 | 
  3049 |       participatingClubIds.forEach((clubId) => {
  3050 |         batch.set(
  3051 |           doc(
  3052 |             db,
  3053 |             "clubs",
  3054 |             clubId,
  3055 |             "incomingChallenges",
  3056 |             pairingId
  3057 |           ),
  3058 |           {
  3059 |             status: "rejected",
  3060 |             rejectedByClubId: activeClubId,
  3061 |             rejectedByClubName: activeClubName,
  3062 | 
  3063 |             respondedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  3064 |             respondedAtMs: nowMs,
  3065 | 
  3066 |             updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  3067 |             updatedAtMs: nowMs,
  3068 |           },
  3069 |           { merge: true }
  3070 |         );
  3071 |       });
  3072 | 
  3073 |       await batch.commit();
  3074 |     } catch (err) {
  3075 |       console.error(
  3076 |         "[EntryPage] Failed rejecting platform pairing:",
  3077 |         err
  3078 |       );
  3079 | 
  3080 |       window.alert(
  3081 |         "Could not reject this platform challenge."
  3082 |       );
  3083 |     }

  3090 |   const handleAcceptChallenge = async (challenge) => {
  3091 |     if (
  3092 |       challenge?.source === "platform_pairing" ||
  3093 |       challenge?.platformPairingId
  3094 |     ) {
  3095 |       await acceptPlatformPairingChallenge(challenge);
  3096 |       return;
  3097 |     }
  3098 | 
  3099 |     if (!challenge?.challengeId) return;
  3100 | 
  3101 |     try {
  3102 |       const fixtureId =
  3103 |         challenge.fixtureId ||
  3104 |         `challenge_${String(
  3105 |           challenge.challengeId || Date.now()
  3106 |         ).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_")}`;
  3107 | 
  3108 |       const participatingClubIds = Array.from(
  3109 |         new Set(
  3110 |           [
  3111 |             challenge.challengerClubId,
  3112 |             activeClubId,
  3113 |           ]
  3114 |             .map((value) => String(value || "").trim())
  3115 |             .filter(Boolean)
  3116 |         )
  3117 |       );
  3118 | 
  3119 |       const acceptedPayload = {
  3120 |         ...challenge,
  3121 | 
  3122 |         targetClubId: activeClubId,
  3123 |         targetClubName: activeClubName,
  3124 |

  3131 |       };
  3132 |

  3135 |

  3137 |         challengeId: challenge.challengeId || "",
  3138 | 
  3139 |         status: "confirmed",
  3140 |         signupStatus: "open",
  3141 | 
  3142 |         homeClubId: challenge.challengerClubId || "",
  3143 |         homeClubName: challenge.challengerClubName || "Home Club",
  3144 |

  3149 |           challenge.challengerClubLogo ||

  3176 |         createdAtMs: Date.now(),

  3178 |         updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  3179 |         updatedAtMs: Date.now(),
  3180 |       };

  3182 |       const batch = writeBatch(db);
  3183 |

  3186 |           db,

  3255 |       window.alert("Could not accept challenge.");
